NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS
Fern Benally • Alberto L. Peshlakai •Jason E. Whiting • Daryl Seymore • Dawnafe Whitesinger
“We are Navajo County”
 
NOTICE OF PUBLIC HEARING AND AGENDA
Monday, August 12, 2024 *SPECIAL MEETING
NAVAJO COUNTY GOVERNMENTAL COMPLEX
BOARD OF SUPERVISORS' CHAMBERS
100 EAST CODE TALKERS DRIVE
HOLBROOK, AZ 86025
All supervisors will attend virtually. 
Pursuant to A.R.S. §38-431.02(H), the public body will have physical acces to the meeting place one (1) hour prior
to the start of the meeting.
 
The Board may vote to meet in a closed executive session to discuss certain matters and for legal advice on any item
as indicated on the agenda, pursuant to A.R.S. §38-431.03(A)(3). A copy of the agenda background material
provided to the Board, except for items to be considered in executive session, is available for public inspection on
the Navajo County website, www.navajocountyaz.gov
Join the meeting now 
Meeting ID: 270 103 839 785
Passcode: 36jsxb 
 
Dial in by phone
+1 623-232-2568,,11814118# United States, Phoenix
Find a local number 
Phone conference ID: 118 141 18#
9:00 a.m. Call To Order: 
           
1. RECORDER:  
 
a. Consideration of an amendment to the Canvass of the July 30, 2024, Primary
Election, originally canvassed by the Board of Supervisors on Aug. 7, 2024, to
include one provisional ballot that, upon further investigation, was accepted. 
 
Presented By: Donna Hurstrom
 
2. ADJOURN  
 
Dated: August 9, 2024 NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS

<!-- page 2 -->
Posted: 8:30 a.m. By: MWB               
Melissa W. Buckley - Clerk of the Board

<!-- page 3 -->
Board of Supervisors Special 1. a.        
Meeting Date: 08/12/2024  
Title: Amendment to the Primary July 30, 2024 Canvass
Submitted By: Rayleen Richards, Elections Director
Department: Elections
Presented By: Donna Hurstrom
Motion before the Board:
Consideration of an amendment to the Canvass of the July 30, 2024, Primary Election,
originally canvassed by the Board of Supervisors on Aug. 7, 2024, to include one provisional
ballot that, upon further investigation, was accepted. 
Background:
Pursuant to A.R.S. § 16-642
Canvass of election; postponements
  
The governing body holding an election shall meet and canvass the election not less than
six days nor more than twenty days following the election.
1.
Recommendation: 
Staff recommends approval.

Updated Unofficial Canvass
